# Release facts log

Every change made by the `atlas-release-facts` stream: file, pointer, old -> new, reason, source URL. Rendered from the change log written by the repair tools (`setq`, `setfield`, `newsrc`, `log`).

1376 logged edits: 114 value/text/date edits, 416 source-list/locator/note/confidence edits, 676 quote rewordings with the value unchanged, 170 source-record creations or repairs.

Conventions: `verified_at` is 2026-10-25 on every object whose source was re-read that day. Quote edits are checked verbatim against the fetched source text by the tool that wrote them (the two exceptions flagged `unverified-by-cache` are Nominatim JSON, skipped by the checker, and a printed-typography coordinate in a PDF whose text layer drops the degree signs).

## 5. Not changed, for the integrator

- Merge conflicts with `atlas-release` (tested with `git merge-tree`): `detectors/kamland.json`, `experiments/kamland.json`,
  `experiments/na62.json` (the currency pass reformatted/edited neighbouring text). Re-apply this branch's edits on top of the
  `atlas-release` versions: KamLAND `technology` (13 m balloon inside an 18 m containment vessel, 1,879 PMTs, 3.2 kton water-Cherenkov
  outer detector; source arXiv:hep-ex/0212021), KamLAND experiment `physics_goals/0` and `unique_capabilities/0` quotes, and the NA62
  interval quote (`/intervals/*` text "using data collected in 2021 and 2022 ..."; keep the currency pass's note).
- `sites/kamioka-mozumi-mine-super-kamiokande.json /coordinates` quote is typographically exact on the printed page of NIM A 501
  (p. 420) but the PDF text layer drops the degree and prime signs, so the checker classes it `near`.
- `memberships/atlas.json /members/146` (University of Iowa) was removed: the ATLAS public institutions list (fetched 2026-10-25) names
  Iowa State University only.
- `src:rf02-antares-collaboration-archive` is an Internet Archive snapshot typed `official_experiment_site`; reclassify if archive copies
  should not carry rank 1.
- Quotes whose geometry or map records cite the same sources (data/atlas/geometry, data/atlas/maps) were not touched; 29 of them are
  still `near`/`missing` in the checker output (OSM tag strings, PDF typography); they belong to the geometry streams.
- Sites whose `coordinates.quote` is an address rather than a coordinate (verbatim, but not evidence for the lat/lon):
  `sites/lngs-hall-a|b|c.json`, `sites/malargue-auger.json`, `sites/cern-cast-sr8.json`, `sites/cern-ecn3-north-area.json`,
  `sites/cern-neutrino-platform-ehn1.json`; their confidence is `approximate`/`campus` already.
